<div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-12 span-12"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-61d49363d084c03f7a32eec4"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h1 style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;">&nbsp;Battle Academy Supplement Decks</h1><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Have you and your child have been having a blast with the decks included in one of the official <em>Battle Academy</em> products and want to add a little variety to the mix? Here are a number of new decks designed to be of a similar power level to those contained in the official <em>Battle Academy </em>products. You can purchase individual cards you need at online card sellers like TCGPlayer or Troll &amp; Toad. You can also read more about <a href="/guide/buying-cards">buying cards for your deck</a>. In general, you should expect to pay $10 or less for each of the decks on this page.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">A note: if you or your child may be interested in moving on from the Battle Academy toward more competitive play, consider instead buying one of the more recent League Battle Decks instead of assembling one of the decks below. Like the decks included in the official <em>Battle Academy </em>product, the decks on this page are not constructed in a way that allows them to be especially competitively viable. This makes these decks great for some kitchen table fun, but all of these decks will be severely outclassed by the more ‘meta’ decks more commonly associated with less casual play.</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Vileplume, Gloom, Jumpluff, and Tangela all of which appear on Erika’s teams as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon Red,</em> <em>Pokémon Blue, Pokémon Gold, </em>and <em>Pokémon Silver.</em></p>

  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Hoppip EVS 2<br>2 Skiploom EVS 3<br>2 Jumpluff EVS 4<br>3 Oddish UNB 5<br>3 Gloom CEC 3<br>1 Vileplume UNB 8<br>1 Vileplume-GX CEC 4<br>3 Tangela CEC 5<br>2 Tangrowth CEC 6</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22 </strong><br>4 Roller Skater CEC 203<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Erika’s Hospitality HIF 56<br>1 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>1 Klara CRE 145<br>1 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>1 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187<br>1 Potion SVI 188<br>1 Rare Candy SVI 191</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18 </strong><br>18 Basic Grass Energy SVE 1</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Shaymin UPR 15<br>2 Bounsweet DAA 14<br>2 Steenee DAA 15<br>2 Exeggcute PLF 4<br>2 Phantump RCL 14<br>2 Trevenant FST 17<br>2 Morelull BUS 97<br>2 Shiinotic SUM 17<br>2 Drampa BST 119<br>1 Tsareena V FST 21</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22 </strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Klara CRE 145<br>1 Mallow GRI 127<br>1 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Super Rod PAL 188<br>2 Switch SVI 194</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18 </strong><br>18 Basic Grass Energy SVE 1</p>
</div>

  <!--<p style="font-size: 25px; text-align:center; margin-bottom:0px;">Malamar V</p>-->
  <h2 class="r" style="padding-bottom:0px; margin-bottom:0px; margin-top: 3px;">Blaine</h2>
 <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px; font-variant:small-caps;">Featuring Houndoom V</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Houndoom, which appears on Blaine’s rematch team as a Gym Leader in <em>Pokémon HeartGold</em> and <em>Pokémon SoulSilver</em>, Magcargo and Rapidash, which appear on his team in <em>Pokémon Gold and Pokémon Silver, </em>and Arcanine, which is part of his team in <em>Pokémon Red </em>and <em>Pokémon Blue.</em></p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20<br></strong>3 Growlithe FST 32<br>2 Arcanine FST 33<br>3 Slugma FST 34<br>2 Magcargo FST 35<br>2 Houndour LOT 45<br>2 Houndoom LOT 46<br>2 Ponyta CEC 23<br>2 Rapidash CEC 24<br>1 Oricorio GRI 14<br>1 Torkoal V SSH 24</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22<br></strong>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Welder UNB 189<br>1 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>1 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>3 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18<br></strong>18 Basic Fire Energy SVE 2</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Ninetales, Arcanine, and Centiskorch, all of which appear on Kabu’s team as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon Sword </em>and <em>Pokémon Shield, </em>and also features Torkoal, which appears on his Champion Cup team.</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Growlithe UNB 21<br>2 Arcanine SUM 22<br>3 Sizzlipede FST 46<br>2 Centiskorch CPA 10<br>3 Vulpix SSH 22<br>2 Ninetales SSH 23<br>2 Oricorio GRI 14<br>2 Torkoal CES 29<br>1 Centiskorch V DAA 33</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Blacksmith FLF 88<br>2 Kabu CPA 55<br>2 Sightseer LOT 189<br>1 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>4 Switch SVI 194</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18 </strong><br>18 Basic Fire Energy SVE 2</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Starmie, which appears on Misty’s teams as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon Red and Pokémon Blue, </em>and Golduck and Lapras from her team as a gym leader in<em> Pokémon Gold, Pokémon Silver, and Pokémon Crystal </em>and also features Blastoise from her Pokémon World Tournament team in <em>Pokémon Black 2</em> and <em>Pokémon White 2</em>.</p>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Gyarados, Quagsire, and Floatzel, all of which appear on Crasher Wake’s team as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon Diamond </em>and <em>Pokémon Pearl </em>and also features Poliwrath from his Pokémon World Tournament team in <em>Pokémon Black 2</em> and <em>Pokémon White 2</em>.</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Swinub ASR 31<br>3 Piloswine ASR 32<br>2 Mamoswine ASR 33<br>4 Snorunt SIT 41<br>2 Froslass CRE 36<br>1 Glalie SIT 42<br>2 Snover UNM 41<br>2 Abomasnow UPR 38<br>1 Glaceon V PR-SW 196</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>4 Roller Skater CEC 203<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Candice SIT 152<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Aqua Patch GRI 119<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>18 Basic Water Energy SVE 3</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Luxray, which appears on Volkner’s team as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon Diamond </em>and <em>Pokémon Pearl, </em>and also features Jolteon and Electivire, which appear on his rematch team in <em>Pokémon Platinum</em>.</p>

  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Magnemite FLI 34<br>3 Magneton FLI 35<br>2 Magnezone FLI 36<br>3 Helioptile FLI 37<br>2 Heliolisk FLI 38<br>2 Bunnelby FST 214<br>2 Diggersby FST 215<br>2 Minun FST 90<br>1 Raichu V BRS 45</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Clemont GEN 59<br>1 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>1 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Potion SVI 188<br>2 Switch SVI 194</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>18 Basic Lightning Energy SVE 4</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Geodude FST 135<br>3 Graveler FST 136<br>2 Golem FST 137<br>3 Onix UNM 103<br>2 Steelix UNM 104<br>2 Groudon CEC 113<br>2 Hawlucha BRS 127<br>2 Passimian DAA 97<br>1 Onix-GX HIF 36</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Brock's Grit HIF 53<br>1 Professor’s Research SVI 190<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Potion SVI 188<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>18 Basic Fighting Energy SVE 6</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Amaura and Tyrunt, which appear on Grant’s team as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon X and Pokémon Y, </em>and Aurorus and Tyrantrum, which appear on his Battle Chateau team in <em>Pokémon X </em>and<em> Pokémon Y.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Note</strong>: Unidentified Fossil is not a Basic Pokémon when in the deck or in your hand. You won’t be able to start with Unidentified Fossil when playing Grant’s deck, but you can start with Omastar V or either version of Relicanth!</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16</strong><br>3 Amaura FLI 27<br>3 Relicanth UNM 111<br>2 Anorith SIT 95<br>2 Armaldo CEC 112<br>2 Tyrunt FLI 68<br>1 Aurorus FLI 28<br>1 Tyrantrum FLI 69<br>1 Omastar V SIT 35<br>1 Relicanth SIT 44</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 26</strong><br>4 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Sonia CPA 65<br>1 Klara CRE 145<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>4 Unidentified Fossil SIT 165<br>2 Fossil Excavation Map FLI 107<br>2 Switch SVI 194</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>9 Basic Water Energy SVE 3<br>9 Basic Fighting Energy SVE 6</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Galarian Zigzagoon SSH 117<br>3 Galarian Linoone FST 160<br>2 Galarian Obstagoon FST 161<br>3 Scraggy SLG 50<br>2 Scrafty CPA 42<br>3 Stunky RCL 114<br>2 Skuntank RCL 115<br>1 Darkrai DAA 105<br>1 Malamar V RCL 121</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Piers CPA 58<br>1 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Potion SVI 188<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18 </strong><br>18 Basic Darkness Energy SVE 7</p>
</div>

 <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px; font-variant:small-caps;">Featuring Umbreon V</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Eevee SHF 52<br>3 Honchkrow LOR 115<br>3 Murkrow SIT 106<br>2 Absol ASR 97<br>2 Sneasel CES 86<br>2 Spiritomb UNB 112<br>2 Umbreon LOT 120<br>2 Weavile CEC 44<br>1 Umbreon V EVS 94</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>4 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>3 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187<br>2 Switch SVI 194</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>18 Basic Darkness Energy SVE 7</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Magnemite and Steelix which appear on Jasmine’s team as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon Gold</em> and <em>Pokémon Silver </em>and Magnezone, Metagross, Skarmory, and Bronzong, which appear on her rematch team in <em>Pokémon HeartGold </em>and <em>Pokémon SoulSilver.</em></p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Axew BRS 110<br>3 Fraxure BRS 111<br>2 Haxorus BRS 112<br>3 Trapinch BRS 74<br>3 Vibrava BRS 75<br>2 Flygon BRS 76<br>2 Drampa BST 119<br>1 Drampa CEC 159<br>1 Flygon V BRS 106</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Klara CRE 145<br>1 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Energy Search SSH 161<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Potion SVI 188</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>9 Basic Fighting Energy SVE 6<br>5 Basic Metal Energy SVE 8<br>4 Basic Grass Energy SVE 1</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Noibat BUS 109<br>2 Noivern FLI 101<br>3 Skrelp CEC 91<br>2 Dragalge CEC 92<br>2 Deino EVS 113<br>2 Zweilous CIN 61<br>2 Hydreigon DAA 110<br>2 Drampa CEC 159<br>1 Drampa BST 119<br>1 Noivern V EVS 117</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Copycat EVS 143<br>2 Judge FST 235<br>2 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>12 Basic Darkness Energy SVE 7<br>6 Basic Psychic Energy SVE 5</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Pidgey and Pidgeotto which appear on Falkner’s team as a gym leader in <em>Pokémon Gold</em> and <em>Pokémon Silver </em>and Noctowl, Honchkrow, Pidgeot, and Pelipper, which appear on his rematch team in <em>Pokémon HeartGold </em>and <em>Pokémon SoulSilver.</em></p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Swablu DRM 56<br>1 Altaria SIT 143<br>1 Altaria-GX DRM 41<br>3 Taillow GRI 103<br>2 Swellow GRI 104<br>3 Wingull RCL 41<br>2 Pelipper SUM 38<br>2 Hoothoot BUS 106<br>2 Noctowl ASR 121<br>1 Shaymin UPR 111</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>3 Youngster SVI 198<br>2 Winona ROS 96<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Potion SVI 188<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>12 Basic Water Energy SVE 3<br>6 Basic Fairy Energy SVE 9</p>
</div>

    <div id="lenora" class="deck_box">

  <h2 class="c" style="padding-bottom:0px; margin-bottom:0px; margin-top: 3px;">Lenora</h2>
 <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px; font-variant:small-caps;">Featuring Stoutland V</p>
</div>
  
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This deck features Stoutland, the fully evolved form of Herdier, which appears on Lenora’s team as a Gym Leader in <em>Pokémon Black </em>and <em>Pokémon White</em>, and<em> </em>Braviary, Dunsparce, Sawsbuck, Kangaskhan, and Lickilicky, which appear on her Pokémon World Tournament teams in <em>Pokémon Black 2 </em>and <em>Pokémon White 2</em>.</p>
</div>

  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Deerling CEC 15<br>2 Sawsbuck CEC 16<br>3 Lickitung UPR 102<br>2 Lickilicky UNM 162<br>2 Kangaskhan DAA 133<br>2 Rufflet EVS 136<br>2 Braviary EVS 137<br>2 Shaymin UPR 111<br>1 Dunsparce FST 207<br>1 Stoutland V BST 117</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22</strong><br>4 Nemona SVI 180<br>4 Bug Catcher FST 226<br>4 Youngster SVI 198<br>1 Sonia CPA 65<br>4 Great Ball PAL 183<br>2 Potion SVI 188<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pokémon Catcher SVI 187</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18</strong><br>16 Basic Grass Energy SVE1<br>2 Powerful Colorless Energy DAA 176</p>
